<div class="fcb-iframe fcb-iframe--center  ">
    
    
    <h2 class="fcb-iframe__title">Transforming the future with commercial finance</h2>

    

    <div class="fcb-iframe__iframe-container">
        
        
        <div class="fcb-iframe__iframe-wrapper fcb-iframe__iframe-wrapper--wistia-video">
            <span class="sr-only">Transforming the future with commercial finance video</span>
            <script src="https://fast.wistia.com/player.js" async=""></script><script src="https://fast.wistia.com/embed/b0812dybpt.js" async="" type="module"></script><style>wistia-player[media-id='b0812dybpt']:not(:defined) { background: center / contain no-repeat url('https://fast.wistia.com/embed/medias/b0812dybpt/swatch'); display: block; filter: blur(5px); padding-top:56.25%; }</style> <wistia-player media-id="b0812dybpt" aspect="1.7777777777777777" data-wistia-tracking="processed" unique-id="wistia-b0812dybpt-16"></wistia-player>
        </div>
    </div>

    
        <div class="fcb-collapse fcb-collapse--slide fcb-collapse--ease-out fcb-iframe__collapse">
            <button class="fcb-collapse__trigger fcb-iframe__transcript-link" id="fcb-collapse__trigger--0" aria-controls="fcb-collapse__content--0" aria-expanded="false">
                Transcript
                <svg aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" class="fcb-icon fcb-icon--sm fcb-icon--sys-chevron-thick fcb-iframe__arrow" data-trackable="true" data-asset-id="sys-chevron-thick">
                    <use xlink:href="/etc.clientlibs/firstcitizens/clientlibs/clientlib-aem/resources/icons/icons.svg#sys-chevron-thick"></use>
                </svg>
            </button>
            <div class="fcb-collapse__content" id="fcb-collapse__content--0" aria-labelledby="fcb-collapse__trigger--0" style="display: none;">
                <div class="fcb-rte fcb-iframe__transcript-content">
                    <p>Imagine if a bank could help businesses harness the power of the sun.</p>
<p>Keep cargo flowing in the international supply chain.</p>
<p>Give young children access to the health care they need. Keep the public safe from cyber threats.</p>
<p>At First Citizens Bank, we're committed to making a difference on a deeper level with financial insights and industry knowledge to supercharge the future for the better. That's why we focus on helping businesses build lasting financial security that grows with them. Our commercial finance team lives and breathes that vision every day across multiple sectors that touch on all aspects of society.</p>
<p>We put our knowledge and insight to work for a range of customer needs and our core values to set us apart from other lenders. So how do we deliver financial security that grows with our clients?</p>
<p>We bring that future vision to life with a fully customized and industry specific approach. Aerospace, defense and government services, commercial real estate, energy, health care, maritime, sponsor finance, tech and telecom, and media and entertainment.</p>
<p>Meanwhile, our comprehensive lending products span a range of industries, asset based and cash flow solutions, as well as real estate financing, factoring and receivables management solutions, and capital equipment finance.</p>
<p>And by focusing on middle market investments, we work to unleash the growth potential that ultimately drives our economy.</p>
<p>Imagine how powerful that can be. You see, we really do have it covered.</p>
<p>So whether we're financing projects that keep your plane on its flight path, enable lifesaving medical procedures, harness the power of <abbr>AI</abbr>, or simply allow your cell phone to communicate, our commercial finance team is helping transform the future and growing with you step by step.</p>

                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

(function () {
  console.log("🛠️ Starting smart Wistia player observer...");

  window.videos = window.videos || {};
  window._wq = window._wq || [];

  const trackProgress = (step, videoData) => {
    if (!videoData.name && videoData.playerInstance) {
      videoData.name = videoData.playerInstance.name();
    }

    console.log(`📈 Progress: ${step} for video "${videoData.name}" (${videoData.id})`);
    videoData.milestone = step;
    if (typeof _satellite !== "undefined") {
      const { playerInstance, ...dataForAdobe } = videoData;
      _satellite.track("video progress", dataForAdobe);
    }
  };

  const resetProgressOnComplete = (videoId) => {
    if (!window.videos[videoId]) return;
    window.videos[videoId].status = {
      step_play: false, step_25: false, step_50: false,
      step_75: false, step_95: false, step_end: false
    };
  };

  const checkProgress = (videoId, milestone, percent) => {
    const step = "step_" + milestone;
    const videoData = window.videos[videoId];
    if (!videoData) return;

    if (milestone === "play" || milestone === "end") {
      if (!videoData.status[step]) {
        videoData.status[step] = true;
        trackProgress(step, videoData);
      }
      if (milestone === "end") {
        resetProgressOnComplete(videoId);
      }
    } else {
      if ((percent * 100) >= milestone && !videoData.status[step]) {
        videoData.status[step] = true;
        trackProgress(step, videoData);
      }
    }
  };

  const initializeVideoTracking = (video) => {
    const videoId = video.hashedId();
    if (!videoId || window.videos?.[videoId]?.bound) return;

    console.log("✅ Tracking initialized for video:", videoId);

    window.videos[videoId] = {
      id: videoId,
      name: video.name() || '',
      duration: video.duration(),
      status: {
        step_play: false, step_25: false, step_50: false,
        step_75: false, step_95: false, step_end: false
      },
      bound: true,
      playerInstance: video
    };

    let timePlayed = 0;

    video.bind("play", () => checkProgress(videoId, "play"));

    video.bind("percentwatchedchanged", (percent) => {
      checkProgress(videoId, 25, percent);
      checkProgress(videoId, 50, percent);
      checkProgress(videoId, 75, percent);
      checkProgress(videoId, 95, percent);
    });

    video.bind("timechange", (t) => { timePlayed = t; });

    video.bind("pause", () => {
      console.log(`⏸ Paused "${video.name()}" at:`, timePlayed);
      if (typeof alloy !== "undefined") {
        alloy("sendEvent", {
          eventType: "video.pause",
          xdm: {
            media: {
              name: video.name(),
              timePlayed: timePlayed
            }
          }
        });
      }
    });

    video.bind("end", () => {
      console.log(`🏁 Ended "${video.name()}" at:`, timePlayed);
      checkProgress(videoId, "end");
    });
  };

  const tryBindModernPlayerViaWistiaAPI = (mediaId, attempts = 0) => {
    const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 20;
    const INTERVAL_MS = 300;

    const poll = () => {
      const player = window.Wistia?.api?.(mediaId);
      if (player && typeof player.bind === "function") {
        console.log(`✅ Wistia.api("${mediaId}") is ready`);
        initializeVideoTracking(player);
      } else if (attempts < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
        setTimeout(() => tryBindModernPlayerViaWistiaAPI(mediaId, attempts + 1), INTERVAL_MS);
      } else {
        console.warn(`⏱️ Timed out waiting for Wistia.api("${mediaId}")`);
      }
    };

    poll();
  };

  const findAndInitWistiaPlayers = () => {
    const players = document.querySelectorAll('wistia-player[media-id], [data-wistia-id]');

    players.forEach(playerElement => {
      if (playerElement.dataset.wistiaTracking === 'processed') return;
      playerElement.dataset.wistiaTracking = 'processed';

      const isModern = playerElement.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'wistia-player';
      const mediaId = playerElement.getAttribute(isModern ? "media-id" : "data-wistia-id");
      if (!mediaId) return;

      console.log(`🎬 Found Wistia player (${isModern ? "Modern" : "Legacy"}): ${mediaId}`);

      if (isModern) {
        tryBindModernPlayerViaWistiaAPI(mediaId);
      } else {
        window._wq.push({
          id: mediaId,
          onReady: function (video) {
            console.log(`🎉 Legacy Player API is ready for ${mediaId} via _wq!`);
            initializeVideoTracking(video);
          }
        });
      }
    });
  };

  let debounceTimer;
  const debouncedInit = () => {
    clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
    debounceTimer = setTimeout(findAndInitWistiaPlayers, 300);
  };

  const observer = new MutationObserver(debouncedInit);
  observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", debouncedInit);
  setTimeout(findAndInitWistiaPlayers, 500);
})();

</script>

<script>
(function () {
   'use strict';
   function insertAfter(targetLinkSelector, html, duplicateCheckSelector) {
        const targetLink = document.querySelector(
            targetLinkSelector
        );
        if (!targetLink) {
            return;
        }
        if (
            duplicateCheckSelector &&
            document.querySelector(duplicateCheckSelector)
        ) {
            return;
        }
        const targetLi = targetLink.closest(
            '.fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link-wrapper--page'
        );
        if (!targetLi) {
            return;
        }
        targetLi.insertAdjacentHTML(
            'afterend',
            html
        );
    }
    function addFundBanking() {
        insertAfter(
            'a[href="/commercial/solutions/credit-financing"]',
            `
            <li class="fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link-wrapper fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link-wrapper--page">
                <a href="https://fundbanking.firstcitizens.com"
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    class="fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link--page fcb-global-nav-header-hook-page-title"
                    data-analytics="Fund Banking">
                    Fund Banking
                </a>
            </li>
            `,
            'a[data-analytics="Fund Banking"]'
        );
    }
    function addInnovationBanking() {
        insertAfter(
            'a[href="/commercial/expertise/healthcare"]',
            `
            <li class="fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link-wrapper fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link-wrapper--page">
                <a href="https://innovation.firstcitizens.com"
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    class="fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link fcb-global-nav-header__main-nav-link--page fcb-global-nav-header-hook-page-title"
                    data-analytics="Innovation Banking">
                    Innovation Banking
                </a>
            </li>
            `,
            'a[data-analytics="Innovation Banking"]'
        );
    }
    function init() {
        addFundBanking();
        addInnovationBanking();
    }
    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener(
            'DOMContentLoaded',
            init
        );
    } else {
        init();
    }
})();

</script>

(function() {
  console.log('CVP tracking is starting...');
  var storageKey = 'cvp_channel_stack';
  var cookieName = 'cvp_channel_stack';
  var firstTouchKey = 'cvp_first_touch_channel';
  var maxLength = 10;
  var expiryDays = 30;
  var sessionTTLMinutes = 30;
  var now = Date.now();
  var url = new URL(window.location.href);
  var referrer = document.referrer;
  var hostname = location.hostname.replace(/^www\./, '');

  function normalize(str) {
    return (str || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  }

  var utmMedium = normalize(url.searchParams.get('utm_medium'));
  var utmCampaign = normalize(url.searchParams.get('utm_campaign'));
  var gclid = url.searchParams.has('gclid');
  var msclkid = url.searchParams.has('msclkid');
  var fbclid = url.searchParams.has('fbclid');
  var channel = '';

  var searchEngines = /(google|bing|yahoo|duckduckgo|baidu|aol|ask|yandex|naver|ecosia|startpage|search|searchalot|lycos|searx)\./;
  var socialSites = /(facebook|linkedin|twitter|youtube|reddit|tiktok|instagram|tumblr|snapchat|pinterest|vimeo|flickr|x\.com|threads\.net|whatsapp|telegram)\./;
  var videoSites = /(youtube|vimeo|twitch)\./;
  var shoppingSites = /(amazon|ebay|shopify|walmart|etsy|target|aliexpress|bestbuy|homedepot|lowes|costco|kroger|macys|nordstrom)\./;
  var aiAssistants = /(chat\.openai\.com|chatgpt\.com|claude\.ai|copilot\.microsoft\.com|chat\.com)/;
  var aiSearchEngines = /(perplexity\.ai|meta\.ai|bard\.google\.com|gemini\.google\.com|mistral\.ai)/;
  var pushMedium = /(sms|push|mobile-push)/;
  var crossNetworkCampaign = /performance_max|cross-network/i;

  if (utmMedium === 'ppc' || utmMedium === 'cpc' || gclid || msclkid) {
    channel = 'Paid Search';
  } else if (utmMedium === 'display') {
    channel = 'Display';
  } else if (utmMedium === 'email') {
    channel = 'Email';
  } else if (utmMedium === 'paid-social') {
    channel = 'Paid Social';
  } else if (utmMedium === 'video') {
    channel = 'Video';
  } else if (utmMedium === 'audio') {
    channel = 'Audio';
  } else if (utmMedium === 'social') {
    channel = 'Social';
  } else if (utmMedium === 'fcb' || utmMedium === 'referral') {
    channel = 'Digital Banking Banners';
  } else if (utmMedium === 'print') {
    channel = 'Print';
  } else if (utmMedium && !channel) {
    channel = 'Unassigned';
  } else if (utmCampaign.includes(':affiliate:')) {
    channel = 'Affiliate';
  }

  var refDomain = referrer ? (new URL(referrer)).hostname.replace(/^www\./, '') : '';
  var refQuery = referrer ? (new URL(referrer)).search : '';

  if (!channel) {
    if (shoppingSites.test(refDomain) || utmMedium.includes('shopping')) {
      channel = utmMedium.match(/cpc|ppc|paid|shop/) ? 'Paid Shopping' : 'Organic Shopping';
    } else if (videoSites.test(refDomain)) {
      channel = utmMedium.match(/cpc|ppc|paid/) ? 'Paid Video' : 'Organic Video';
    } else if (pushMedium.test(utmMedium)) {
      channel = 'Mobile Push Notifications';
    } else if (crossNetworkCampaign.test(utmCampaign)) {
      channel = 'Cross-Network Ads';
    } else if (!utmMedium) {
      if (refDomain.match(searchEngines) && /[?&](q|query|p)=/i.test(refQuery)) {
        channel = 'Organic Search';
      } else if (refDomain.match(socialSites)) {
        channel = 'Organic Social';
      } else if (refDomain.match(aiSearchEngines)) {
        channel = 'Organic AI Search';
      } else if (refDomain.match(aiAssistants)) {
        channel = 'AI Assistant';
      }
    } else {
      if (refDomain.match(searchEngines)) {
        channel = 'Paid Search';
      } else if (refDomain.match(socialSites)) {
        channel = 'Paid Social';
      }
    }

    if (!channel && !referrer) {
      channel = 'Direct (Typed/Bookmarked)';
    } else if (!channel && refDomain === hostname) {
      channel = 'Internal Referrer';
    } else if (!channel && refDomain) {
      channel = 'Referring Domains';
    }
  }

  function setCookie(name, value, days) {
    var expires = '';
    if (days) {
      var d = new Date();
      d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days * 86400000));
      expires = '; expires=' + d.toUTCString();
    }
    document.cookie = name + '=' + encodeURIComponent(value) + expires + '; path=/; domain=' + hostname;
  }

  function getCookie(name) {
    var match = document.cookie.match('(^|;)\\s*' + name + '\\s*=\\s*([^;]+)');
    return match ? decodeURIComponent(match.pop()) : null;
  }

  if (!getCookie(firstTouchKey)) {
    try {
      setCookie(firstTouchKey, channel + '>' + now, 730);
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  var data = [];
  try {
    data = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(storageKey)) || [];
  } catch (e) {
    try {
      var cookieVal = getCookie(cookieName);
      if (cookieVal) data = JSON.parse(cookieVal);
    } catch (e2) {
      data = [];
    }
  }

  var lastTouch = data.find(function(item) {
    return item[0] === channel;
  });

  var shouldAdd = true;
  if (lastTouch) {
    var lastAgeMinutes = (now - lastTouch[1]) / 60000;
    if (lastAgeMinutes < sessionTTLMinutes) {
      shouldAdd = false;
    }
  }

  if (shouldAdd) {
    data.push([channel, now]);
  }

  data = data.slice(-maxLength).filter(function(item) {
    var age = Math.round((now - item[1]) / 86400000);
    return age <= expiryDays;
  });

  try { localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(data)); } catch (e) {}
  try { setCookie(cookieName, JSON.stringify(data), expiryDays); } catch (e) {}

  var result = data.map(function(item) {
    var ch = item[0];
    var ts = item[1];
    var age = Math.round((now - ts) / 86400000);
    return { channel: ch, age: age };
  });

  var flattened = result.map(function(item) {
    return item.channel + '>' + item.age;
  }).join('::');

  window.cvpChannel = channel;
  window.cvpChannelHistory = result;
  window.cvp_with_recency = flattened;

  sessionStorage.setItem('cvp_channel', JSON.stringify(result));
  sessionStorage.setItem('cvp_with_recency', JSON.stringify(flattened));

  console.log('CVP channel is ' + channel);
  console.log('CVP with recency: ' + flattened);
})();

  });
})();

/* Return Launch event detail */
function getEventDetail(eventName) {
  var event = {};
  adobeDataLayer.launchevents.forEach(function(e) {
    event = (e.eventName == eventName) ? e : {};
  });
  return event;
}

/* Return an element from the event payload */
function getPayloadDetail(node) {
    try {
        var data = eval("event.detail." + node);
        if(typeof(event) != "undefined" && typeof(event.detail) != "undefined" && typeof(data) != "undefined" && data != null) {
            if(typeof(data) == "string") {
              return data.trim();
            } else {
              return data;
            }
        } else {
          return "";
        }
    } catch(e) {
        _satellite.logger.warn("unable to retrieve payload detail for node "+node+": " + e.message);
        return null;
    }
}

var getComponentRoot = function(e) {
    var compname = null;
    if(typeof(e) != "undefined") {
        compname = e.getAttribute("data-component");
        if(compname && compname != "") {
            return e;
        } else {
            e = e.closest("[data-component]:not([data-component=''])");
            if(typeof(e) != "undefined") {
                compname = e.getAttribute("data-component");
                if(compname && compname != "") {
                    return e;
                } else {
                    return false;
                }
            } else {
                return false;
            }
        }
    } else {
        return false;
    }
}
var getComponentName = function(e) {
    return e.getAttribute("data-component").replaceAll(",", "").replaceAll(":", "").replaceAll('"', "");
}
var getComponentDescription = function(e) {
    var compdesc = e.getAttribute("data-analytics");
    if(compdesc == "" || compdesc == null) {
        compdesc = e.querySelector("[data-analytics]").getAttribute("data-analytics").replaceAll(",", "").replaceAll(":", "").replaceAll('"', "");
    }
    if(compdesc == "" || compdesc == null) {
        return "unknown name";
    } else {
        return compdesc.replaceAll(",", "").replaceAll(":", "").replaceAll('"', "");
    }
}
var getComponentDetails = function(e) {
    e = getComponentRoot(e);
    return getComponentName(e) + ":" + getComponentDescription(e);
}
